update in .env with correct domain/path\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        super().__init__()\n        if self.server_env is None:\n            self.server_env = environ.get(\"ENV\", \"local\")\n        self.server_env = self.server_env.lower()\n\n    def is_local(self):\n        return self.server_env == \"local\"\n    \n    def is_test(self):\n        return self.server_env == \"test\"\n    \n    def is_prod(self):\n        return self.server_env == \"production\"\n\n\npvf_settings = PvfGlobalSettings()","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":343,"totalLines":343,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py","time":{"start":1787317652666,"end":1787317654074}},"id":"prt_0246f0b65001dFmHyqzm4O50fi","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","messageID":"msg_0246ebef6002NtUWnnhSuHzDw8"}}
{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787317654143,"sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call-4444901c-a54e-4890-9565-e0ef03ed53e7-8","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/AGENTS.md"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/AGENTS.md</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: # AGENTS.md\n2: \n3: Python 3.13 + FastAPI + SQLModel + Alembic (Postgres). Nuxt 3 SPA in `client/` builds into `static_client/` (gitignored, published locally) served by FastAPI.\n4: \n5: The application runs on **pvf** (`src/pvf/`, the Pratt Ventures Framework): a standard\n6: runner (`src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py`) reads `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`, establishes the\n7: DB connection, composes four FastAPI apps (noauth `/auth-ws`, session `/ws`, share\n8: `/ext-ws`, external `/api`), and invokes the application shell (`src/app_shell.py`,\n9: name/entry point configured in the YAML). Framework entry points are documented in\n10: `src/pvf/README.md`; non-REST services come from `src/pvf/utils/pvf_services.py`.\n11: \n12: **User-facing product language:** `docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md` (Project · Option · Factor · Compare · Results · share types). Prefer those terms in UI copy and API `description=` strings; do not rename DB/JSON fields unless explicitly requested.\n13: \n14: Root `README.md` is the product and layout overview (including pvf isolation). Prefer this file for engineering commands, testing, and OpenAPI.\n15: \n16: ## pvf is off-limits (unless explicitly allowed)\n17: \n18: **Hard rule.** pvf (`src/pvf/`) is an infrastructure provider. PowerChoice is the application we are working on. Do **not** change pvf infrastructure or its dedicated settings — including `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` and `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py` — unless the user **explicitly** allows it.\n19: \n20: Application work belongs in the PowerChoice application area. Interact with pvf only through `src/app_shell.py` (via `PvfInvocation`) and the modules in `src/pvf/bindings`. Do not reach into pvf internals.\n21: \n22: Unless specifically asked otherwise, treat pvf as read-only infrastructure.\n23: \n24: ## Engineering standards\n25: \n26: Every change is production work by and for senior engineers. Build reliable, defensive, resilient systems. Users will not tolerate flakiness, and we cannot reach most of their environments — robustness and self-healing are required in every plane.\n27: \n28: Understand the existing design before changing it. Prefer the simplest clean architecture that fully solves the problem. Preserve strong abstractions. Do not add technical debt, duplication, shortcuts, unnecessary complexity, or speculative work.\n29: \n30: Handle edge cases, errors, compatibility, usability, and visual polish. Match existing conventions. Keep complexity local and factoring effective. Make confident changes with minimal unintended impact.\n31: \n32: Verify. Run relevant tests; add tests for new or corrected behavior. Inspect failures — never bypass them. Exercise important user flows end-to-end.\n33: \n34: Do not stop at “it works.” Ship code that is clear, maintainable, robust, secure, performant, and product-ready.\n35: \n36: ## Priority: request intake (before build actions)\n37: \n38: **High priority.** Before implementing or changing code, evaluate every request for:\n39: \n40: - **Scope** — what is in vs out; avoid expanding beyond what was asked\n41: - **Duplication** — existing APIs, UI, settings, models, or flows that already cover the need\n42: - **Risk** — tenancy/auth, data loss, breaking API/UI contracts, migrations, published `static_client/`, shared DB tests\n43: \n44: If anything is ambiguous, incomplete, or high-risk, **stop and ask for clarification or guidance** before build actions (edits, migrations, generate, deploys). Prefer a short check-in over speculative implementation.\n45: \n46: ## Environment & toolchain\n47: \n48: - **Poetry** lives in `src/` (`package-mode = false`, in-project venv → **`src/.venv`**). `cd src && poetry install`. Devcontainer puts `src/.venv/bin` on `PATH`.\n49: - Deployment-fixed startup config is **`src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`** (shell module/entry point, env file name, feature switches, share vocabularies). Feature switches are injected into `pvf_settings` read-only at startup; only the startup logic can set them.\n50: - Runtime config file is **`.env`** at the repo root (ingested via python-dotenv by the runner/alembic config + pydantic-settings; process env overrides the file). Template: `example.env`. Gitignore: `*.env` with `!example.env`.\n51: - Key vars: `SERVER_ENV` (`local` | `staging` | `production` → `settings.server_env`; `is_prod()` only for `production`), `DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING`, `BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA`, `STRIPE_ENDPOINT_SECRET_PRIMARY`, `STRIPE_HOOK_STR_PRIMARY`, `PLATFORM_LINUX`.\n52: - No project lint/typecheck tooling. Verify with pytest + running the server.\n53: - Imports mix `from src...` and package-relative forms → **repo root must be on `PYTHONPATH`**. VS Code Alembic launch sets it; backend launch uses `cwd: src` and may need `PYTHONPATH=${workspaceFolder}` if `from src...` fails.\n54: \n55: ## Commands\n56: \n57: ```bash\n58: cd src && poetry install\n59: \n60: # API from repo root (activates venv, fastapi dev 0.0.0.0:8100 via pvf_app_runner)\n61: ./start-server.sh\n62: # manual: source src/.venv/bin/activate && fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py --host 0.0.0.0\n63: \n64: # Migrations (scripts cd into src/, use ./.venv/bin/alembic; env.py is a thin shim over\n65: # src/pvf/pvf_get_alembic_config.py, which forces BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA=False)\n66: ./scripts/migrations/run-migrations.sh\n67: ./scripts/migrations/generate-migration.sh \"<message>\"\n68: ./scripts/migrations/rollback-one-migration.sh   # sets PYTHONPATH to repo root\n69: ./scripts/migrations/list-migration-heads.sh\n70: ./scripts/migrations/merge-migration-heads.sh\n71: \n72: # Tests from repo root — hit the real configured DB (not isolated)\n73: src/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/\n74: src/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/test_auth.py -k <name>\n75: \n76: # Browser e2e (Playwright) — starts/reuses API :8100 + Nuxt :3000\n77: cd e2e && npm install && npx playwright install chromium\n78: # First time / missing shared libs on Linux: npx playwright install-deps chromium\n79: cd e2e && npm test\n80: # Share access-mode suite only:\n81: cd e2e && npm run test:share\n82: # Modules map to OpenAPI tags: auth, admin, manage-projects, manage-project-content,\n83: # api-keys, dashboard, self-reg, context, project-votes, stripe hooks, external API, etc.\n84: ```\n85: \n86: Fresh local: `poetry install` → `./scripts/migrations/run-migrations.sh` → `./start-server.sh`.\n87: \n88: ## Frontend (`client/`)\n89: \n90: - Nuxt 3 SPA (`ssr: false`) + Vue 3 + Vuetify. Session JWT cookie `access_token` (httponly) against the UI API.\n91: - Dev: API on :8100, then `cd client && npm install && npm run dev` (:3000). Vite proxies `/auth-ws`, `/ws`, `/ext-ws`, `/api`, `/status` → `:8100`. CORS allows `localhost:3000` with credentials. In Nuxt dev, browser calls are same-origin so the proxy owns cookies.\n92: - Ship UI with API: `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh` → `nuxt generate` with empty `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE`, copies `.output/public` → **`static_client/`** (gitignored; published locally, not committed).\n93: - SPA generate gotcha: `client/nuxt.config.ts` maps Vite `{ entry }` → `{ entry, server }` **only when `nuxt.options.dev`**. Do not apply during `generate` or entry CSS is dropped (script guards this).\n94: - **After any change under `client/`**, rebuild and publish the UI: run `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh` (generates + copies into `static_client/`, the artifact FastAPI actually serves). The build must complete successfully before considering the work done; fix build/SFC errors and do not leave a broken generate or a stale `static_client/`.\n95: \n96: ## Postgres dev sidecar\n97: \n98: `.devcontainer/docker-compose.yml` Postgres 16:\n99: \n100: - Host bind-mount **must exist** before compose starts: `mkdir -p /workspaces/powerchoice/postgres-data-${USER}` (compose interpolates `USER` per tenant so co-tenants on the same VM get separate DB volumes).\n101: - In-container DSN: `postgresql+psycopg://powerchoice:powerchoice_dev_password@postgres:5432/powerchoice`.\n102: - SQLite (`sqlite:///local.db`) only auto-`create_all` when not prod **and** `BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA`. Real schema changes go through Alembic. `*.db` is gitignored.\n103: \n104: ## Architecture\n105: \n106: **`src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py`** composes the root app plus four FastAPI sub-apps, and\n107: invokes **`src/app_shell.py`** (the application bridge configured in the YAML) with a\n108: `PvfInvocation`:\n109: \n110: | App | Mount | Auth | Role |\n111: |-----|-------|------|------|\n112: | `app` (root) | `/` | — | Mounts others + static + SPA fallback + Stripe hooks; `/status`, `/alive` |\n113: | `app_noauth` | `/auth-ws` | none | Login, password reset, self-registration |\n114: | `app_session` | `/ws` | `SessionDep` + `UserAccessDep` (JWT cookie) | Session UI API |\n115: | `app_share` | `/ext-ws` | `SessionDep` + share cookie/magic-key gate | Share link access |\n116: | `app_external` | `/api` | `SessionDep` + `WebServiceDep` (signed headers) | Customer toolkit API |\n117: \n118: - Routes are relative to each app's mount prefix (external URLs unchanged: `/auth-ws/...`, `/ws/...`, `/ext-ws/...`, `/api/...`). Stripe hooks keep full paths on the root app.\n119: - DI is strictly enforced at composition time by the runner's boot integrity check (`src/pvf/utils/dependency_integrity.py`), independent of endpoint signatures. `test-helpers` routes (tagged, non-prod only) are exempt.\n120: - Reserved prefixes return JSON 404 (no SPA fallback): `/ws`, `/ext-ws`, `/api`, `/auth`, `/hook-stripe-events`, `/api/webhooks`. Other 404s serve `static_client/index.html`.\n121: - Aggregate session-surface OpenAPI at `/session/docs.json` (root + noauth + session + share); external at `/api/openapi.json`.\n122: - Static: `/static` → `static/`; UI assets → `static_client/`. Paths resolved from workspace root (not cwd).\n123: - Application hooks (entity resolver, share email dispatch/matrix/enrichers, webhook payload builders/readiness) are registered in `src/app_shell.py::register_app_hooks`, shared with the watcher process.\n124: - **`src/powerchoice_watcher.py`**: separate process polling DB for API callback delivery (generic machinery in `src/pvf/utils/callback_delivery.py`). Prod/staging via supervisord (`build/supervisor.conf`); **not** started by `./start-server.sh`. Launch config: module `src.powerchoice_watcher`. Supervisor runs `./start-job-runner.sh`, which is **not in-repo** — check deploy packaging before relying on it.\n125: - Models: framework tables in `src/pvf/db/models/`; application tables in `src/db/models/`; `bootstrap.py` imports app models and calls `pvf_bootstrap.import_all_models()` so Alembic/metadata see all tables. New app models must be wired there.\n126: - Schema extensions: applications can add columns to pvf tables (`customer`, `user`, `sharelink`, `apiaccessconfiguration`) via `register_schema_extensions` in the shell — see `src/pvf/README.md`.\n127: \n128: ## Stripe webhooks\n129: \n130: - Real routes: `POST /hook-stripe-events/{hook_str}` and sandbox variant. `hook_str` is `STRIPE_HOOK_STR_PRIMARY` or a per-customer hook id.\n131: - `./scripts/stripe/stripe-forward.sh` forwards to a **stale path** (`/stripe-events-...`). Point the CLI at `/hook-stripe-events/<hook_str>` instead.\n132: \n133: ## Build & deploy\n134: \n135: - **App version:** On each build (any shippable change set), always increment the last segment of `settings.VERSION` in `src/config/config_settings.py` (`a.b.LAST` → `a.b.LAST+1`). Exposed via `GET /status` (`version`) and the UI About box.\n136: - `build/build.sh` (repo root): image `powerchoice-service:vX.Y.Z`, patch bump from `build/.last-build` (Docker tag only; still bump `settings.VERSION` as above).\n137: - Runtime: supervisord runs `fastapi run src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py` + watcher. `build/run.sh` sources `~/.bashrc` then supervisord.\n138: - `build/compose.yml`: staging-style sample (`powerchoice:v0.5.5`, host `9001`→`8100`); bind paths are placeholders.\n139: \n140: ## Testing gotchas\n141: \n142: - Layout: `src/tests/conftest.py` + tag-cluster modules + `src/tests/helpers/` (`as_user`/`login`/`signed_api_headers`, factories, best-effort cleanup).\n143: - `conftest` sets `settings.PYTEST_ACTIVE = True` and clears SendGrid **before** importing `app`. `PYTEST_ACTIVE` skips the 500 handler so pytest shows full tracebacks. Autouse mock patches SendGrid delivery.\n144: - Settings overrides in tests: pvf-owned fields must be set on **both** `settings` (app instance) and `pvf_settings` (what pvf internals read); conftest's helper loop shows the pattern.\n145: - The app is composed by `src.pvf.pvf_app_runner` (conftest imports `app` from there); the shell runs during that import, so YAML, env, DB, and hooks are live for tests.\n146: - Fixtures create random customers/users/apps/API keys and soft-delete/deactivate on teardown. Residual log/event rows are expected. **Not hermetic** against a shared dev DB.\n147: - Assert security failure modes explicitly (unauth, non-admin, non-sysadmin, cross-tenant, bad API signatures).\n148: - **Material new functionality must ship with tests** (happy path + security/tenancy failure modes) in the matching tag-cluster module under `src/tests/` (or a new one). Prefer API-level tests via `TestClient` and existing helpers over one-off DB scripts.\n149: - Before and after large updates, run the affected suite (or full `src/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/`) against the configured DB and fix regressions before considering the change done. Share/vote coverage lives in `test_share_manage.py`, `test_share_ext_access.py`, `test_project_votes.py`.\n150: - **After tests**, if any endpoint in that surface changed, regenerate the matching OpenAPI snapshot at the repo root (see **OpenAPI snapshots** below).\n151: \n152: ## OpenAPI snapshots\n153: \n154: Repo-root contract files used to define endpoints for the client and other services:\n155: \n156: | File | Surface | Live schema |\n157: |------|---------|-------------|\n158: | `openapi_sessions.json` | Session UI API (root + noauth + session + share) | `/session/docs.json` |\n159: | `openapi_api.json` | Customer toolkit API (external app under `/api`) | `/api/openapi.json` |\n160: \n161: The extraction script builds the app in-process via `src.pvf.pvf_app_runner` and\n162: prefix-stitches the sub-app schemas (the sessions file covers all browser-facing\n163: surfaces). Operation IDs are regenerated from mount-relative route paths.\n164: \n165: Regenerate **only the file whose endpoints changed** (path, method, params, request/response models, or tags), after the related tests pass:\n166: \n167: ```bash\n168: ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh           # both\n169: ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh sessions  # openapi_sessions.json\n170: ./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh api       # openapi_api.json\n171: ```\n172: \n173: Non-prod snapshots include `test_helpers` routes (those routers are not registered when `is_prod()`).\n174: \n175: ## Git remote policy (important)\n176: \n177: This repo uses its own `origin` for storage — there is **no** local bare sibling repo. Verify with `git remote -v` (should be like `git@gh-powerchoice:Pratt-Ventures/power-choice.git`, never a `d:\\...` local path) and `git ls-remote origin`.\n178: \n179: Host bind mounts often show `root:root` while the container user is `ubuntu` → Git “dubious ownership”. The Dockerfile sets `safe.directory` for `/workspaces/powerchoice`. If `git ls-remote origin` still fails, add that path with `git config --global --add safe.directory /workspaces/powerchoice`.\n\n(End of file - total 179 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# AGENTS.md\n\nPython 3.13 + FastAPI + SQLModel + Alembic (Postgres). Nuxt 3 SPA in `client/` builds into `static_client/` (gitignored, published locally) served by FastAPI.\n\nThe application runs on **pvf** (`src/pvf/`, the Pratt Ventures Framework): a standard\nrunner (`src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py`) reads `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`, establishes the\nDB connection, composes four FastAPI apps (noauth `/auth-ws`, session `/ws`, share\n`/ext-ws`, external `/api`), and invokes the application shell (`src/app_shell.py`,\nname/entry point configured in the YAML). Framework entry points are documented in\n`src/pvf/README.md`; non-REST services come from `src/pvf/utils/pvf_services.py`.\n\n**User-facing product language:** `docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md` (Project · Option · Factor · Compare · Results · share types). Prefer those terms in UI copy and API `description=` strings; do not rename DB/JSON fields unless explicitly requested.\n\nRoot `README.md` is the product and layout overview (including pvf isolation). Prefer this file for engineering commands, testing, and OpenAPI.\n\n## pvf is off-limits (unless explicitly allowed)\n\n**Hard rule.** pvf (`src/pvf/`) is an infrastructure provider. PowerChoice is the application we are working on. Do **not** change pvf infrastructure or its dedicated settings — including `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` and `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py` — unless the user **explicitly** allows it.\n\nApplication work belongs in the PowerChoice application area. Interact with pvf only through `src/app_shell.py` (via `PvfInvocation`) and the modules in `src/pvf/bindings`. Do not reach into pvf internals.","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/AGENTS.md","text":"# AGENTS.md\n\nPython 3.13 + FastAPI + SQLModel + Alembic (Postgres). Nuxt 3 SPA in `client/` builds into `static_client/` (gitignored, published locally) served by FastAPI.\n\nThe application runs on **pvf** (`src/pvf/`, the Pratt Ventures Framework): a standard\nrunner (`src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py`) reads `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`, establishes the\nDB connection, composes four FastAPI apps (noauth `/auth-ws`, session `/ws`, share\n`/ext-ws`, external `/api`), and invokes the application shell (`src/app_shell.py`,\nname/entry point configured in the YAML). Framework entry points are documented in\n`src/pvf/README.md`; non-REST services come from `src/pvf/utils/pvf_services.py`.\n\n**User-facing product language:** `docs_examples/terminology_dictionary.md` (Project · Option · Factor · Compare · Results · share types). Prefer those terms in UI copy and API `description=` strings; do not rename DB/JSON fields unless explicitly requested.\n\nRoot `README.md` is the product and layout overview (including pvf isolation). Prefer this file for engineering commands, testing, and OpenAPI.\n\n## pvf is off-limits (unless explicitly allowed)\n\n**Hard rule.** pvf (`src/pvf/`) is an infrastructure provider. PowerChoice is the application we are working on. Do **not** change pvf infrastructure or its dedicated settings — including `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` and `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py` — unless the user **explicitly** allows it.\n\nApplication work belongs in the PowerChoice application area. Interact with pvf only through `src/app_shell.py` (via `PvfInvocation`) and the modules in `src/pvf/bindings`. Do not reach into pvf internals.\n\nUnless specifically asked otherwise, treat pvf as read-only infrastructure.\n\n## Engineering standards\n\nEvery change is production work by and for senior engineers. Build reliable, defensive, resilient systems. Users will not tolerate flakiness, and we cannot reach most of their environments — robustness and self-healing are required in every plane.\n\nUnderstand the existing design before changing it. Prefer the simplest clean architecture that fully solves the problem. Preserve strong abstractions. Do not add technical debt, duplication, shortcuts, unnecessary complexity, or speculative work.\n\nHandle edge cases, errors, compatibility, usability, and visual polish. Match existing conventions. Keep complexity local and factoring effective. Make confident changes with minimal unintended impact.\n\nVerify. Run relevant tests; add tests for new or corrected behavior. Inspect failures — never bypass them. Exercise important user flows end-to-end.\n\nDo not stop at “it works.” Ship code that is clear, maintainable, robust, secure, performant, and product-ready.\n\n## Priority: request intake (before build actions)\n\n**High priority.** Before implementing or changing code, evaluate every request for:\n\n- **Scope** — what is in vs out; avoid expanding beyond what was asked\n- **Duplication** — existing APIs, UI, settings, models, or flows that already cover the need\n- **Risk** — tenancy/auth, data loss, breaking API/UI contracts, migrations, published `static_client/`, shared DB tests\n\nIf anything is ambiguous, incomplete, or high-risk, **stop and ask for clarification or guidance** before build actions (edits, migrations, generate, deploys). Prefer a short check-in over speculative implementation.\n\n## Environment & toolchain\n\n- **Poetry** lives in `src/` (`package-mode = false`, in-project venv → **`src/.venv`**). `cd src && poetry install`. Devcontainer puts `src/.venv/bin` on `PATH`.\n- Deployment-fixed startup config is **`src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`** (shell module/entry point, env file name, feature switches, share vocabularies). Feature switches are injected into `pvf_settings` read-only at startup; only the startup logic can set them.\n- Runtime config file is **`.env`** at the repo root (ingested via python-dotenv by the runner/alembic config + pydantic-settings; process env overrides the file). Template: `example.env`. Gitignore: `*.env` with `!example.env`.\n- Key vars: `SERVER_ENV` (`local` | `staging` | `production` → `settings.server_env`; `is_prod()` only for `production`), `DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING`, `BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA`, `STRIPE_ENDPOINT_SECRET_PRIMARY`, `STRIPE_HOOK_STR_PRIMARY`, `PLATFORM_LINUX`.\n- No project lint/typecheck tooling. Verify with pytest + running the server.\n- Imports mix `from src...` and package-relative forms → **repo root must be on `PYTHONPATH`**. VS Code Alembic launch sets it; backend launch uses `cwd: src` and may need `PYTHONPATH=${workspaceFolder}` if `from src...` fails.\n\n## Commands\n\n```bash\ncd src && poetry install\n\n# API from repo root (activates venv, fastapi dev 0.0.0.0:8100 via pvf_app_runner)\n./start-server.sh\n# manual: source src/.venv/bin/activate && fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py --host 0.0.0.0\n\n# Migrations (scripts cd into src/, use ./.venv/bin/alembic; env.py is a thin shim over\n# src/pvf/pvf_get_alembic_config.py, which forces BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA=False)\n./scripts/migrations/run-migrations.sh\n./scripts/migrations/generate-migration.sh \"<message>\"\n./scripts/migrations/rollback-one-migration.sh   # sets PYTHONPATH to repo root\n./scripts/migrations/list-migration-heads.sh\n./scripts/migrations/merge-migration-heads.sh\n\n# Tests from repo root — hit the real configured DB (not isolated)\nsrc/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/\nsrc/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/test_auth.py -k <name>\n\n# Browser e2e (Playwright) — starts/reuses API :8100 + Nuxt :3000\ncd e2e && npm install && npx playwright install chromium\n# First time / missing shared libs on Linux: npx playwright install-deps chromium\ncd e2e && npm test\n# Share access-mode suite only:\ncd e2e && npm run test:share\n# Modules map to OpenAPI tags: auth, admin, manage-projects, manage-project-content,\n# api-keys, dashboard, self-reg, context, project-votes, stripe hooks, external API, etc.\n```\n\nFresh local: `poetry install` → `./scripts/migrations/run-migrations.sh` → `./start-server.sh`.\n\n## Frontend (`client/`)\n\n- Nuxt 3 SPA (`ssr: false`) + Vue 3 + Vuetify. Session JWT cookie `access_token` (httponly) against the UI API.\n- Dev: API on :8100, then `cd client && npm install && npm run dev` (:3000). Vite proxies `/auth-ws`, `/ws`, `/ext-ws`, `/api`, `/status` → `:8100`. CORS allows `localhost:3000` with credentials. In Nuxt dev, browser calls are same-origin so the proxy owns cookies.\n- Ship UI with API: `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh` → `nuxt generate` with empty `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE`, copies `.output/public` → **`static_client/`** (gitignored; published locally, not committed).\n- SPA generate gotcha: `client/nuxt.config.ts` maps Vite `{ entry }` → `{ entry, server }` **only when `nuxt.options.dev`**. Do not apply during `generate` or entry CSS is dropped (script guards this).\n- **After any change under `client/`**, rebuild and publish the UI: run `./scripts/nuxt/update_static_client.sh` (generates + copies into `static_client/`, the artifact FastAPI actually serves). The build must complete successfully before considering the work done; fix build/SFC errors and do not leave a broken generate or a stale `static_client/`.\n\n## Postgres dev sidecar\n\n`.devcontainer/docker-compose.yml` Postgres 16:\n\n- Host bind-mount **must exist** before compose starts: `mkdir -p /workspaces/powerchoice/postgres-data-${USER}` (compose interpolates `USER` per tenant so co-tenants on the same VM get separate DB volumes).\n- In-container DSN: `postgresql+psycopg://powerchoice:powerchoice_dev_password@postgres:5432/powerchoice`.\n- SQLite (`sqlite:///local.db`) only auto-`create_all` when not prod **and** `BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA`. Real schema changes go through Alembic. `*.db` is gitignored.\n\n## Architecture\n\n**`src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py`** composes the root app plus four FastAPI sub-apps, and\ninvokes **`src/app_shell.py`** (the application bridge configured in the YAML) with a\n`PvfInvocation`:\n\n| App | Mount | Auth | Role |\n|-----|-------|------|------|\n| `app` (root) | `/` | — | Mounts others + static + SPA fallback + Stripe hooks; `/status`, `/alive` |\n| `app_noauth` | `/auth-ws` | none | Login, password reset, self-registration |\n| `app_session` | `/ws` | `SessionDep` + `UserAccessDep` (JWT cookie) | Session UI API |\n| `app_share` | `/ext-ws` | `SessionDep` + share cookie/magic-key gate | Share link access |\n| `app_external` | `/api` | `SessionDep` + `WebServiceDep` (signed headers) | Customer toolkit API |\n\n- Routes are relative to each app's mount prefix (external URLs unchanged: `/auth-ws/...`, `/ws/...`, `/ext-ws/...`, `/api/...`). Stripe hooks keep full paths on the root app.\n- DI is strictly enforced at composition time by the runner's boot integrity check (`src/pvf/utils/dependency_integrity.py`), independent of endpoint signatures. `test-helpers` routes (tagged, non-prod only) are exempt.\n- Reserved prefixes return JSON 404 (no SPA fallback): `/ws`, `/ext-ws`, `/api`, `/auth`, `/hook-stripe-events`, `/api/webhooks`. Other 404s serve `static_client/index.html`.\n- Aggregate session-surface OpenAPI at `/session/docs.json` (root + noauth + session + share); external at `/api/openapi.json`.\n- Static: `/static` → `static/`; UI assets → `static_client/`. Paths resolved from workspace root (not cwd).\n- Application hooks (entity resolver, share email dispatch/matrix/enrichers, webhook payload builders/readiness) are registered in `src/app_shell.py::register_app_hooks`, shared with the watcher process.\n- **`src/powerchoice_watcher.py`**: separate process polling DB for API callback delivery (generic machinery in `src/pvf/utils/callback_delivery.py`). Prod/staging via supervisord (`build/supervisor.conf`); **not** started by `./start-server.sh`. Launch config: module `src.powerchoice_watcher`. Supervisor runs `./start-job-runner.sh`, which is **not in-repo** — check deploy packaging before relying on it.\n- Models: framework tables in `src/pvf/db/models/`; application tables in `src/db/models/`; `bootstrap.py` imports app models and calls `pvf_bootstrap.import_all_models()` so Alembic/metadata see all tables. New app models must be wired there.\n- Schema extensions: applications can add columns to pvf tables (`customer`, `user`, `sharelink`, `apiaccessconfiguration`) via `register_schema_extensions` in the shell — see `src/pvf/README.md`.\n\n## Stripe webhooks\n\n- Real routes: `POST /hook-stripe-events/{hook_str}` and sandbox variant. `hook_str` is `STRIPE_HOOK_STR_PRIMARY` or a per-customer hook id.\n- `./scripts/stripe/stripe-forward.sh` forwards to a **stale path** (`/stripe-events-...`). Point the CLI at `/hook-stripe-events/<hook_str>` instead.\n\n## Build & deploy\n\n- **App version:** On each build (any shippable change set), always increment the last segment of `settings.VERSION` in `src/config/config_settings.py` (`a.b.LAST` → `a.b.LAST+1`). Exposed via `GET /status` (`version`) and the UI About box.\n- `build/build.sh` (repo root): image `powerchoice-service:vX.Y.Z`, patch bump from `build/.last-build` (Docker tag only; still bump `settings.VERSION` as above).\n- Runtime: supervisord runs `fastapi run src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py` + watcher. `build/run.sh` sources `~/.bashrc` then supervisord.\n- `build/compose.yml`: staging-style sample (`powerchoice:v0.5.5`, host `9001`→`8100`); bind paths are placeholders.\n\n## Testing gotchas\n\n- Layout: `src/tests/conftest.py` + tag-cluster modules + `src/tests/helpers/` (`as_user`/`login`/`signed_api_headers`, factories, best-effort cleanup).\n- `conftest` sets `settings.PYTEST_ACTIVE = True` and clears SendGrid **before** importing `app`. `PYTEST_ACTIVE` skips the 500 handler so pytest shows full tracebacks. Autouse mock patches SendGrid delivery.\n- Settings overrides in tests: pvf-owned fields must be set on **both** `settings` (app instance) and `pvf_settings` (what pvf internals read); conftest's helper loop shows the pattern.\n- The app is composed by `src.pvf.pvf_app_runner` (conftest imports `app` from there); the shell runs during that import, so YAML, env, DB, and hooks are live for tests.\n- Fixtures create random customers/users/apps/API keys and soft-delete/deactivate on teardown. Residual log/event rows are expected. **Not hermetic** against a shared dev DB.\n- Assert security failure modes explicitly (unauth, non-admin, non-sysadmin, cross-tenant, bad API signatures).\n- **Material new functionality must ship with tests** (happy path + security/tenancy failure modes) in the matching tag-cluster module under `src/tests/` (or a new one). Prefer API-level tests via `TestClient` and existing helpers over one-off DB scripts.\n- Before and after large updates, run the affected suite (or full `src/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/`) against the configured DB and fix regressions before considering the change done. Share/vote coverage lives in `test_share_manage.py`, `test_share_ext_access.py`, `test_project_votes.py`.\n- **After tests**, if any endpoint in that surface changed, regenerate the matching OpenAPI snapshot at the repo root (see **OpenAPI snapshots** below).\n\n## OpenAPI snapshots\n\nRepo-root contract files used to define endpoints for the client and other services:\n\n| File | Surface | Live schema |\n|------|---------|-------------|\n| `openapi_sessions.json` | Session UI API (root + noauth + session + share) | `/session/docs.json` |\n| `openapi_api.json` | Customer toolkit API (external app under `/api`) | `/api/openapi.json` |\n\nThe extraction script builds the app in-process via `src.pvf.pvf_app_runner` and\nprefix-stitches the sub-app schemas (the sessions file covers all browser-facing\nsurfaces). Operation IDs are regenerated from mount-relative route paths.\n\nRegenerate **only the file whose endpoints changed** (path, method, params, request/response models, or tags), after the related tests pass:\n\n```bash\n./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh           # both\n./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh sessions  # openapi_sessions.json\n./scripts/extract_openapi/update_openapi_json.sh api       # openapi_api.json\n```\n\nNon-prod snapshots include `test_helpers` routes (those routers are not registered when `is_prod()`).\n\n## Git remote policy (important)\n\nThis repo uses its own `origin` for storage — there is **no** local bare sibling repo. Verify with `git remote -v` (should be like `git@gh-powerchoice:Pratt-Ventures/power-choice.git`, never a `d:\\...` local path) and `git ls-remote origin`.\n\nHost bind mounts often show `root:root` while the container user is `ubuntu` → Git “dubious ownership”. The Dockerfile sets `safe.directory` for `/workspaces/powerchoice`. If `git ls-remote origin` still fails, add that path with `git config --global --add safe.directory /workspaces/powerchoice`.","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":179,"totalLines":179,"truncated":false}},"title":"AGENTS.md","time":{"start":1787317653665,"end":1787317654088}},"id":"prt_0246f0f20001mdiiZHmu7yNwRu","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","messageID":"msg_0246ebef6002NtUWnnhSuHzDw8"}}

shutil","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/pvf_get_alembic_config.py","text":"\"\"\"pvf_get_alembic_config — alembic database configuration orchestration.\n\nWhen alembic needs the database configuration it invokes get_alembic_runtime(), which\nreads pvf_app_startup.yaml and orchestrates the steps to export the current\nSQLAlchemy/SQLModel database configuration:\n\n  1. load the startup YAML (env file name, application models module)\n  2. ingest the env file (alembic always runs as its own process)\n  3. apply startup-owned values to pvf_settings and disable sample-data bootstrap\n     (alembic owns the schema; create_all must not run)\n  4. import ALL pvf model tables (the migrated schema stays complete regardless of\n     which features are activated at runtime) plus the application's models_module\n  5. return the merged metadata + database URL\n\nOnly the paths needed for a proper current schema are loaded — no routers, no FastAPI\napplications, no mail/stripe runtime wiring.\n\"\"\"\nimport importlib\nimport os\nimport shutil\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import MetaData\n\nfrom .bindings.pvf_startup_config import PvfStartupConfig, apply_startup_config, load_startup_config\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass PvfAlembicRuntime:\n    target_metadata: MetaData\n    db_url: str\n\n\ndef get_alembic_runtime(config_path: str | None = None) -> PvfAlembicRuntime:\n    startup_config: PvfStartupConfig = load_startup_config(config_path)\n\n    # env file ingestion (fresh process for alembic invocations)\n    from dotenv import load_dotenv\n\n    env_path = Path(startup_config.env_file)\n    if not env_path.is_absolute():\n        env_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / env_path  # pvf -> src -> workspace root\n    if env_path.is_file():\n        load_dotenv(env_path, override=False)\n\n    from .config.pvf_config_settings import pvf_settings\n\n    apply_startup_config(startup_config, pvf_settings)\n    # Force set this to false so that we don't auto-create a local db. We want Alembic to do that.\n    pvf_settings.BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA = False\n\n    # Application schema extensions must be declared before any model import, exactly\n    # as the runner does, so autogenerate sees the same metadata.\n    from .db.model_factory import get_schema_extension_registry\n\n    shell_module = importlib.import_module(startup_config.application.shell_module)\n    register_schema_extensions = getattr(shell_module, \"register_schema_extensions\", None)\n    if callable(register_schema_extensions):\n        register_schema_extensions(get_schema_extension_registry())\n\n    # All framework tables, so the migrated schema is complete even for features that\n    # are deactivated at runtime.\n    from .db.models import pvf_bootstrap\n\n    pvf_bootstrap.import_all_models()\n\n    # The application's table specifications, as named in the startup YAML.\n    models_module = importlib.import_module(startup_config.application.models_module)\n\n    get_target_metadata = getattr(models_module, \"get_target_metadata\", None)\n    if callable(get_target_metadata):\n        target_metadata = get_target_metadata()\n    else:\n        from sqlmodel import SQLModel\n\n        target_metadata = SQLModel.metadata\n\n    return PvfAlembicRuntime(target_metadata=target_metadata, db_url=pvf_settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING)\n\n\ndef run_migrations_offline(context, runtime: PvfAlembicRuntime) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run migrations in 'offline' mode: emit SQL without a database connection.\"\"\"\n    context.configure(\n        url=runtime.db_url,\n        target_metadata=runtime.target_metadata,\n        literal_binds=True,\n        dialect_opts={\"paramstyle\": \"named\"},\n    )\n\n    with context.begin_transaction():\n        context.run_migrations()\n\n\ndef run_migrations_online(context, runtime: PvfAlembicRuntime) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run migrations in 'online' mode against the configured engine.\n\n    FastAPI is run from the root directory and looks for the sqlite db there by default.\n    However, alembic is run from the src directory, and creates / migrates files there.  Check\n    if the current working directory is 'src' and the database is SQLite.  If so, copy the db file\n    from the parent directory to the current working directory, run migrations, and then move it back.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .db.connect import DatabaseConnection\n\n    connectable = DatabaseConnection.get_engine()\n\n    cwd = os.getcwd()\n    if os.path.basename(cwd) == 'src' and runtime.db_url.startswith('sqlite'):\n        db_file = runtime.db_url.split('/')[-1]\n        parent_dir = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(cwd, '..'))\n        src_db_path = os.path.join(cwd, db_file)\n        parent_db_path = os.path.join(parent_dir, db_file)\n\n        # Copy the SQLite database file to the current working directory if it exists in the parent directory\n        if os.path.exists(parent_db_path):\n            shutil.copy(parent_db_path, src_db_path)\n\n        with connectable.connect() as connection:\n            context.configure(connection=connection, target_metadata=runtime.target_metadata)\n            with context.begin_transaction():\n                context.run_migrations()\n\n        # Copy the SQLite database file back to the parent directory\n        if os.path.exists(src_db_path):\n            shutil.move(src_db_path, parent_db_path)\n    else:\n        with connectable.connect() as connection:\n            context.configure(connection=connection, target_metadata=runtime.target_metadata)\n            with context.begin_transaction():\n                context.run_migrations()","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":130,"totalLines":130,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/pvf_get_alembic_config.py","time":{"start":1787317653243,"end":1787317654106}},"id":"prt_0246f0ea5001qNiBpWz5KH5Ynt","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","messageID":"msg_0246ebef6002NtUWnnhSuHzDw8"}}

Model metadata must be registered (pvf_bootstrap + the\n17:     application's models module) before get_engine() when SQLite create_all is in play.\n18:     \"\"\"\n19:     db: ClassVar[Engine | None] = None\n20: \n21:     @staticmethod\n22:     def get_engine() -> Engine:\n23:         if DatabaseConnection.db is None:\n24:             if not settings.is_prod():\n25:                 print(f\"Connecting database: {settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING}\")\n26: \n27:             DatabaseConnection.db = create_engine(settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING, echo=settings.DB_ECHO_SQL_TO_CONSOLE)\n28: \n29:             # In the case we're using a SQLite DB, perform migrations.  Otherwise these will be performed using\n30:             # Alembic.\n31:             if \"sqlite\" in settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING and settings.is_prod() is False and settings.BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA:\n32:                 SQLModel.metadata.create_all(DatabaseConnection.db)\n33:         return DatabaseConnection.db\n\n(End of file - total 33 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"from __future__ import annotations\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\nfrom typing import ClassVar\n\nfrom sqlmodel import SQLModel, create_engine\nfrom sqlalchemy import Engine\n\nfrom ..config.pvf_config_settings import pvf_settings as settings\n\n\nclass DatabaseConnection(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Process-global database engine holder.\n\n    The pvf startup logic (pvf_app_runner / pvf_get_alembic_config) establishes the\n    connection first and hands the handle to both pvf internals and the application\n    via PvfInvocation. Model metadata must be registered (pvf_bootstrap + the\n    application's models module) before get_engine() when SQLite create_all is in play.\n    \"\"\"\n    db: ClassVar[Engine | None] = None\n","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/db/connect.py","text":"from __future__ import annotations\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\nfrom typing import ClassVar\n\nfrom sqlmodel import SQLModel, create_engine\nfrom sqlalchemy import Engine\n\nfrom ..config.pvf_config_settings import pvf_settings as settings\n\n\nclass DatabaseConnection(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Process-global database engine holder.\n\n    The pvf startup logic (pvf_app_runner / pvf_get_alembic_config) establishes the\n    connection first and hands the handle to both pvf internals and the application\n    via PvfInvocation. Model metadata must be registered (pvf_bootstrap + the\n    application's models module) before get_engine() when SQLite create_all is in play.\n    \"\"\"\n    db: ClassVar[Engine | None] = None\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def get_engine() -> Engine:\n        if DatabaseConnection.db is None:\n            if not settings.is_prod():\n                print(f\"Connecting database: {settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING}\")\n\n            DatabaseConnection.db = create_engine(settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING, echo=settings.DB_ECHO_SQL_TO_CONSOLE)\n\n            # In the case we're using a SQLite DB, perform migrations.  Otherwise these will be performed using\n            # Alembic.\n            if \"sqlite\" in settings.DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING and settings.is_prod() is False and settings.BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA:\n                SQLModel.metadata.create_all(DatabaseConnection.db)\n        return DatabaseConnection.db","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":33,"totalLines":33,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/db/connect.py","time":{"start":1787317653113,"end":1787317654123}},"id":"prt_0246f0cdf001m4yt0igEXmqqaY","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","messageID":"msg_0246ebef6002NtUWnnhSuHzDw8"}}

\"\"\"pvf_app_runner — the standard, executable application runner for pvf-based applications.\n2: \n3: Startup orchestration:\n4:   1. load pvf_app_startup.yaml (bindings, feature switches, share vocabularies)\n5:   2. ingest the configured env file (usually .env) with python-dotenv\n6:   3. inject startup-owned values into pvf_settings (read-only thereafter)\n7:   4. establish the database connection FIRST; on failure, clear diagnostics to stderr\n8:      and exit with an error code\n9:   5. register pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n10:   6. build the four FastAPI applications (noauth / session / share / external) and the\n11:      root app; register pvf-owned routers\n12:   7. invoke the application shell entry point with a PvfInvocation; it registers hooks\n13:      and routers and returns None (success), an error message, or raises\n14:   8. complete setup: include application routers, merge OpenAPI tag metadata, run the\n15:      dependency-injection integrity check, install handlers, mount apps and static files\n16: \n17: Run with:  fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py   (or fastapi run ... for production)\n18: The module-level `app` is the composed ASGI 1).\",\n175:             EXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG,\n176:         )\n177: \n178:     # 4. database connection first\n179:     from .db.connect import DatabaseConnection\n180: \n181:     db = import:\n185:     # import the shell module (declarations only; no models at module level by\n186:     # contract) and let it register added columns on pvf tables\n187:     try:\n188: register_schema_extensions = getattr(shell_module, \"register_schema_extensions\", None)\n197:     if callable(register_schema_extensions):\n198:         register_schema_extensions(get_schema_extension_registry())\n199: \n200:     # 5. pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n201:     from .db.models import pvf_bootstrap\n202: \n203: on failure, clear diagnostics to stderr\n     and exit with an error code\n  5. register pvf model metadata (feature-gated)\n  6. build the four FastAPI applications (noauth / session / share / external) and the\n     root app; register pvf-owned routers\n  7. invoke the application shell entry point with a PvfInvocation; it registers hooks\n     and routers and returns None (success), an error message, or raises\n  8. complete setup: include application routers, merge OpenAPI tag metadata, run the\n     dependency-injection integrity check, install handlers, mount apps and static files\n\nRun with:  fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py   (or fastapi run ... for production)\nThe module-level `app` is the composed ASGI root application.\n\"\"\"\nimport importlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom traceback import format_exc\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError\nfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\nfrom fastapi.openapi.docs import get_swagger_ui_html\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\nfrom fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\nfrom sqlalchemy import text\n\n    \nfrom .bindings.pvf_startup_config import (\n    PvfStartupConfig,\n    PvfStartupConfigError,\n    apply_startup_config,\n    load_startup_config,\n)\n\n_WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]  # pvf -> src -> workspace root\n_STATIC_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static\")\n_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR = str(_WORKSPACE_ROOT / \"static_client\")\n\n# Mount prefixes for the four FastAPI applications\nMOUNT_PREFIXES = {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n\n# Non-app paths (reserved prefixes) always return JSON 404s rather than the SPA page\nRESERVED_PREFIXES = [\"/ws\", \"/ext-ws\", \"/api\", \"/auth\", \"/hook-stripe-events\", \"/api/webhooks\"]\n\nEXIT_BAD_STARTUP_CONFIG = 2\nEXIT_DATABASE_FAILED = 3\nEXIT_APP_STARTUP_FAILED = 4\n\n# The composed applications, populated by build_app(); consumed by tooling\n# (OpenAPI snapshot extraction, diagnostics).\ncomposed_apps: dict[str, FastAPI] = {}\n\n_PVF_TAGS = {\n    \"noauth\": [\n        {\"name\": \"auth\", \"description\": \"Services to support authentication\"},\n        {\"name\": \"self-reg\", \"description\": \"Self Registration Services for Initial Signup\"},\n    ],\n    \"session\": [\n        {\"name\": \"admin\", \"description\": \"Services to customer/user administration and customer/user information\"},\n        {\"name\": \"context\", \"description\": \"Services to access the user/customer context and related user information\"},\n        {\"name\": \"devops\", \"description\": \"Services to access and post log events for diagnostic purposes\"},\n    ],\n    \"share\": [],\n    \"external\": [\n        {\"name\": \"access_check\", \"description\": \"Signed API connectivity checks\"},\n    ],\n}\n\n\ndef _fatal(message: str, exit_code: int) -> None:\n    print(f\"FATAL: {message}\", file=sys.stderr)\n    raise SystemExit(exit_code)\n\n\ndef _load_env_file(startup_config: PvfStartupConfig) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Ingest the configured env file (usually .env). Process environment wins.\"\"\"\n    from dotenv import load_dotenv\n\n    env_path = Path(startup_config.env_file)\n    if not env_path.is_absolute():\n        env_path = _WORKSPACE_ROOT / env_path\n    if env_path.is_file():\n        load_dotenv(env_path, override=False)\n\n\ndef _probe_database_or_exit(db, dsn: str) -> None:\n    try:\n        with db.get_engine().connect() as connection:\n            connection.execute(text(\"SELECT 1\"))\n    except Exception as ex:\n        masked_dsn = re.sub(r\"://([^:@/]+):([^@/]+)@\", r\"://\\1:****@\", dsn)\n        _fatal(\n            \"could not establish a connection to the database.\\n\"\n            f\"  DSN: {masked_dsn}\\n\"\n            f\"  error: {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\",\n            EXIT_DATABASE_FAILED,\n        )\n\n\ndef _merge_openapi_specs(title: str, specs: list[tuple[str, FastAPI]]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge (mount_prefix, app) OpenAPI specs into one schema; paths are prefixed.\n\n    Component name collisions are tolerated only when the schemas are identical\n    (shared pvf models); conflicting definitions are a build error.\n    \"\"\"\n    merged: dict | None = None\n    for prefix, sub_app in specs:\n        spec = sub_app.openapi()\n        if merged is None:\n            merged = {\"openapi\": spec[\"openapi\"], \"info\": {\"title\": title, \"version\": spec[\"info\"].get(\"version\", \"\")},\n                      \"paths\": {}, \"components\": {\"schemas\": {}}, \"tags\": []}\n        for path, item in spec.get(\"paths\", {}).items():\n            merged[\"paths\"][prefix + path] = item\n        for name, schema in spec.get(\"components\", {}).get(\"schemas\", {}).items():\n            existing = merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"].get(name)\n            if existing is not None and existing != schema:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Conflicting OpenAPI schema definitions for '{name}' across composed apps\")\n            merged[\"components\"][\"schemas\"][name] = schema\n        seen_tags = {t[\"name\"] for t in merged[\"tags\"]}\n        for tag in spec.get(\"tags\", []):\n            if tag[\"name\"] not in seen_tags:\n                merged[\"tags\"].append(tag)\n                seen_tags.add(tag[\"name\"])\n    return merged or {}\n\n\n_sessions_openapi_cache: dict = {}\n\n\ndef build_sessions_openapi() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"The aggregate session-facing contract: root + specs.append((MOUNT_PREFIXES[target], composed_apps[target]))\n        _sessions_openapi_cache.update(_merge_openapi_specs(\"Session UI API\", specs))\n    return _sessions_openapi_cache\n\n\ndef {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787317754158,"sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call-41ef18a1-9432-4d6c-b19a-69cd06abfcba-24","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/README.md","limit":80},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/README.md</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: # pvf — Pratt Ventures Framework\n2: \n3: A standard framework for multi-tenant FastAPI + SQLModel applications: session-JWT\n4: authentication, customer/user administration, outbound email, share links, external\n5: (signed-header) APIs with webhook callback delivery, Stripe hooks, branding image\n6: storage, structured logging, and schema extension points for application-specific\n7: columns.\n8: \n9: The framework is composed and launched by a single executable runner configured by a\n10: YAML file in the application's src root. Application-specific behavior is bound\n11: through a shell module and hooks — pvf never imports application modules.\n12: \n13: **Stand-up and architecture:** [`PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage.MD`](PVF%20Architecture%2C%20Configuration%20and%20Usage.MD)\n14: — startup sequence, YAML vs env, application handoff, hooks, feature switches,\n15: services, share/external/watcher, and a minimal all-features-on shell. Use that\n16: document to adopt pvf in a new application; this file is the short contract.\n17: \n18: ## Startup configuration: `pvf_app_startup.yaml`; example in framework src/pvf/example_pvf_app_startup.yaml\n19: \n20: Located at the application src root (override with the `PVF_STARTUP_CONFIG` env var):\n21: \n22: ```yaml\n23: application:\n24:   shell_module: src.app_shell        # module loaded as the application bridge\n25:   entry_point: app_startup           # callable receiving the PvfInvocation\n26:   models_module: src.db.models.bootstrap  # imports all app table specs (alembic probe)\n27: env_file: .env                       # ingested with python-dotenv; process env wins\n28: features:\n29:   activate_stripe_integration: false\n30:   activate_branding_image_store: false\n31:   activate_share_links: true\n32:   activate_external_api: true\n33:   activate_customer_communication: true\n34: share:\n35:   object_actions: [not_set, ...]     # share types the application offers\n36:   access_operations: [not_set, ...]  # operations journalable per share\n37: ```\n38: \n39: Feature switches and share vocabularies are deployment-fixed: they are injected into\n40: `pvf_settings` by the startup logic and are **read-only** thereafter — `.env` and\n41: process environment cannot supply them (see `PvfGlobalSettings` startup-owned\n42: ClassVars). Everything else (secrets, URLs, tuning) lives in the env file (usually\n43: `.env`; ship `example.env` in images).\n44: \n45: ## Entry points for applications\n46: \n47: | Entry point | Use |\n48: |---|---|\n49: | `pvf.pvf_app_runner` | The executable server. `fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py` (dev) / `fastapi run ...` (prod). Builds the DB connection first (fail → stderr + exit code), composes four FastAPI apps, invokes the application shell, verifies dependency injection, mounts static/SPA. |\n50: | `pvf.pvf_get_alembic_config` | Invoked by `src/alembic/env.py`. Reads the YAML and exports the current SQLAlchemy/SQLModel metadata + DB URL, loading only schema-relevant paths from pvf and the application. |\n51: | `pvf.utils.pvf_services` | **The single non-REST entry point.** All standard services an application needs outside of REST endpoints: settings merge, mail, logging, tokens, share gate, callback enqueue, schema extension helpers, dependency types. |\n52: | `pvf.pvf_invocation` | `PvfInvocation` (the runner → application contract), `PvfAppTarget`, `PvfHookRegistry`, `PvfResolvedEntity`. |\n53: | `pvf.config.pvf_config_settings` | `PvfGlobalSettings` (base class for the application's settings) and the active `pvf_settings`. |\n54: | `pvf.depends.*` | `SessionDep`, `UserAccessDep` (+ admin variants), `WebServiceDep` for endpoint signatures. |\n55: | `pvf.db.models.*` | Framework table classes (Customer, User, ShareLink, ApiAccessConfiguration, ...) for queries and id references. |\n56: \n57: Other imports or calls into pvf are discouraged.\n58: \n59: ## The application shell contract\n60: \n61: The shell module named in the YAML exposes:\n62: \n63: - `app_startup(invocation: PvfInvocation) -> str | None` — the entry point. Expected to:\n64:   1. build the application settings (typically `GlobalSettings(PvfGlobalSettings)`) and call `merge_pvf_settings_into(app_settings)`\n65:   2. import the application models module (registers table metadata)\n66:   3. perform application startup tasks (template seeding, sample data)\n67:   4. register hooks on `invocation.hooks` (usually via a `register_app_hooks` function, which the watcher process also calls)\n68:   5. add routers via `invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.<target>, router)`\n69:   6. optionally extend `invocation.openapi_tags` and set `application_name`/`application_version`\n70:   7. return `None` on success, an error message on failure, or raise\n71: - `register_schema_extensions(registry)` *(optional)* — declares added columns on pvf\n72:   tables. Called by the runner and by the alembic config **before any model import**.\n73:   The shell module must not import models at module level.\n74: - `register_app_hooks(hooks)` *(convention)* — shared by the server and watcher processes.\n75: \n76: ## The four FastAPI applications\n77: \n78: | App | Mount | Authorization |\n79: |---|---|---|\n80: | noauth | `/auth-ws` | none (login, password reset, self registration) |\n\n(Showing lines 1-80 of 161. Use offset=81 to continue.)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# pvf — Pratt Ventures Framework\n\nA standard framework for multi-tenant FastAPI + SQLModel applications: session-JWT\nauthentication, customer/user administration, outbound email, share links, external\n(signed-header) APIs with webhook callback delivery, Stripe hooks, branding image\nstorage, structured logging, and schema extension points for application-specific\ncolumns.\n\nThe framework is composed and launched by a single executable runner configured by a\nYAML file in the application's src root. Application-specific behavior is bound\nthrough a shell module and hooks — pvf never imports application modules.\n\n**Stand-up and architecture:** [`PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage.MD`](PVF%20Architecture%2C%20Configuration%20and%20Usage.MD)\n— startup sequence, YAML vs env, application handoff, hooks, feature switches,\nservices, share/external/watcher, and a minimal all-features-on shell. Use that\ndocument to adopt pvf in a new application; this file is the short contract.\n\n## Startup configuration: `pvf_app_startup.yaml`; example in framework src/pvf/example_pvf_app_startup.yaml\n\nLocated at the application src root (override with the `PVF_STARTUP_CONFIG` env var):","truncated":true,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/README.md","text":"# pvf — Pratt Ventures Framework\n\nA standard framework for multi-tenant FastAPI + SQLModel applications: session-JWT\nauthentication, customer/user administration, outbound email, share links, external\n(signed-header) APIs with webhook callback delivery, Stripe hooks, branding image\nstorage, structured logging, and schema extension points for application-specific\ncolumns.\n\nThe framework is composed and launched by a single executable runner configured by a\nYAML file in the application's src root. Application-specific behavior is bound\nthrough a shell module and hooks — pvf never imports application modules.\n\n**Stand-up and architecture:** [`PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage.MD`](PVF%20Architecture%2C%20Configuration%20and%20Usage.MD)\n— startup sequence, YAML vs env, application handoff, hooks, feature switches,\nservices, share/external/watcher, and a minimal all-features-on shell. Use that\ndocument to adopt pvf in a new application; this file is the short contract.\n\n## Startup configuration: `pvf_app_startup.yaml`; example in framework src/pvf/example_pvf_app_startup.yaml\n\nLocated at the application src root (override with the `PVF_STARTUP_CONFIG` env var):\n\n```yaml\napplication:\n  shell_module: src.app_shell        # module loaded as the application bridge\n  entry_point: app_startup           # callable receiving the PvfInvocation\n  models_module: src.db.models.bootstrap  # imports all app table specs (alembic probe)\nenv_file: .env                       # ingested with python-dotenv; process env wins\nfeatures:\n  activate_stripe_integration: false\n  activate_branding_image_store: false\n  activate_share_links: true\n  activate_external_api: true\n  activate_customer_communication: true\nshare:\n  object_actions: [not_set, ...]     # share types the application offers\n  access_operations: [not_set, ...]  # operations journalable per share\n```\n\nFeature switches and share vocabularies are deployment-fixed: they are injected into\n`pvf_settings` by the startup logic and are **read-only** thereafter — `.env` and\nprocess environment cannot supply them (see `PvfGlobalSettings` startup-owned\nClassVars). Everything else (secrets, URLs, tuning) lives in the env file (usually\n`.env`; ship `example.env` in images).\n\n## Entry points for applications\n\n| Entry point | Use |\n|---|---|\n| `pvf.pvf_app_runner` | The executable server. `fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py` (dev) / `fastapi run ...` (prod). Builds the DB connection first (fail → stderr + exit code), composes four FastAPI apps, invokes the application shell, verifies dependency injection, mounts static/SPA. |\n| `pvf.pvf_get_alembic_config` | Invoked by `src/alembic/env.py`. Reads the YAML and exports the current SQLAlchemy/SQLModel metadata + DB URL, loading only schema-relevant paths from pvf and the application. |\n| `pvf.utils.pvf_services` | **The single non-REST entry point.** All standard services an application needs outside of REST endpoints: settings merge, mail, logging, tokens, share gate, callback enqueue, schema extension helpers, dependency types. |\n| `pvf.pvf_invocation` | `PvfInvocation` (the runner → application contract), `PvfAppTarget`, `PvfHookRegistry`, `PvfResolvedEntity`. |\n| `pvf.config.pvf_config_settings` | `PvfGlobalSettings` (base class for the application's settings) and the active `pvf_settings`. |\n| `pvf.depends.*` | `SessionDep`, `UserAccessDep` (+ admin variants), `WebServiceDep` for endpoint signatures. |\n| `pvf.db.models.*` | Framework table classes (Customer, User, ShareLink, ApiAccessConfiguration, ...) for queries and id references. |\n\nOther imports or calls into pvf are discouraged.\n\n## The application shell contract\n\nThe shell module named in the YAML exposes:\n\n- `app_startup(invocation: PvfInvocation) -> str | None` — the entry point. Expected to:\n  1. build the application settings (typically `GlobalSettings(PvfGlobalSettings)`) and call `merge_pvf_settings_into(app_settings)`\n  2. import the application models module (registers table metadata)\n  3. perform application startup tasks (template seeding, sample data)\n  4. register hooks on `invocation.hooks` (usually via a `register_app_hooks` function, which the watcher process also calls)\n  5. add routers via `invocation.add_router(PvfAppTarget.<target>, router)`\n  6. optionally extend `invocation.openapi_tags` and set `application_name`/`application_version`\n  7. return `None` on success, an error message on failure, or raise\n- `register_schema_extensions(registry)` *(optional)* — declares added columns on pvf\n  tables. Called by the runner and by the alembic config **before any model import**.\n  The shell module must not import models at module level.\n- `register_app_hooks(hooks)` *(convention)* — shared by the server and watcher processes.\n\n## The four FastAPI applications\n\n| App | Mount | Authorization |\n|---|---|---|\n| noauth | `/auth-ws` | none (login, password reset, self registration) |","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":80,"totalLines":161,"truncated":true}},"title":"src/pvf/README.md","time":{"start":1787317753801,"end":1787317754125}},"id":"prt_024709687001ura2K2sqGTrH2i","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","messageID":"msg_0246f9d49002OhWU5c83IfeEQN"}}
{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787317755022,"sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call-41ef18a1-9432-4d6c-b19a-69cd06abfcba-25","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_pvf_app_runner.py","limit":160},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/tests/test_pvf_app_runner.py</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: \"\"\"Tests for pvf_app_runner: composition, DI integrity enforcement, and startup failure modes.\"\"\"\n2: import os\n3: import subprocess\n4: import sys\n5: from pathlib import Path\n6: \n7: import pytest\n8: from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, FastAPI\n9: \n10: from src.pvf.depends.check_user_session_jwt_dependencies import get_current_user\n11: from src.pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import PvfAppTarget\n12: from src.pvf.utils.dependency_integrity import verify_app_dependency_integrity\n13: \n14: REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n15: PYTHON = REPO_ROOT / \"src\" / \".venv\" / \"bin\" / \"python\"\n16: \n17: \n18: # --- dependency integrity (synthetic apps) ------------------------------------\n19: \n20: def _app_with_route(*, guard=None, tags=None, path=\"/thing\"):\n21:     app = FastAPI()\n22:     router = APIRouter()\n23:     if guard is None:\n24:         @router.get(path, tags=tags or [])\n25:         async def thing():\n26:             return True\n27:     else:\n28:         @router.get(path, tags=tags or [])\n29:         async def thing_guarded(ctx=Depends(guard)):\n30:             return True\n31:     app.include_router(router)\n32:     return app\n33: \n34: \n35: def test_session_app_requires_session_guard():\n36:     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=\"missing required session-JWT dependency\"):\n37:         verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(), \"session\")\n38: \n39: \n40: def test_session_app_accepts_session_guard():\n41:     verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(guard=get_current_user), \"session\")\n42: \n43: \n44: def test_external_app_requires_signed_header_guard():\n45:     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=\"missing required signed-header dependency\"):\n46:         verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(), \"external\")\n47: \n48: \n49: def test_noauth_and_share_apps_forbid_guards():\n50:     for target in (\"noauth\", \"share\"):\n51:         with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=\"must not require\"):\n52:             verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(guard=get_current_user), target)\n53: \n54: \n55: def test_exemptions_skip_status_and_test_helpers():\n56:     verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(path=\"/status\"), \"session\")\n57:     verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(tags=[\"test-helpers\"]), \"session\")\n58: \n59: \n60: # --- composed application (via the running test app) ---------------------------\n61: \n62: def test_four_apps_composed_and_mounted():\n63:     from src.pvf.pvf_app_runner import MOUNT_PREFIXES, composed_apps\n64: \n65:     assert set(composed_apps) == {\"root\", \"noauth\", \"session\", \"share\", \"external\"}\n66:     assert MOUNT_PREFIXES == {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n67:     mounted_prefixes = {getattr(route, \"path\", None) for route in composed_apps[\"root\"].routes}\n68:     for prefix in MOUNT_PREFIXES.values():\n69:         assert prefix in mounted_prefixes\n70: \n71: \n72: def test_sessions_openapi_aggregate_covers_all_browser_surfaces(client):\n73:     spec = client.get(\"/session/docs.json\").json()\n74:     paths = set(spec[\"paths\"])\n75:     assert any(p.startswith(\"/auth-ws/\") for p in paths)\n76:     assert any(p.startswith(\"/ws/\") for p in paths)\n77:     assert any(p.startswith(\"/ext-ws/\") for p in paths)\n78:     assert \"/status\" in paths  # root app surface\n79: \n80: \n81: def test_reserved_prefix_404_is_json_but_spa_fallback_served(client):\n82:     response = client.get(\"/ws/definitely-not-a-route\")\n83:     assert response.status_code == 404\n84:     assert response.headers[\"content-type\"].startswith(\"application/json\")\n85: \n86:     response = client.get(\"/some/client-side/route\")\n87:     assert response.status_code == 200\n88:     assert \"text/html\" in response.headers[\"content-type\"]\n89: \n90: \n91: # --- startup failure modes (subprocess for isolation) ---------------------------\n92: \n93: def _run_runner_subprocess(env_overrides: dict) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\n94:     env = dict(os.environ)\n95:     env[\"PYTHONPATH\"] = str(REPO_ROOT)\n96:     env.update(env_overrides)\n97:     return subprocess.run(\n98:         [str(PYTHON), \"-c\", \"from src.pvf.pvf_app_runner import app\"],\n99:         capture_output=True, text=True, env=env, cwd=REPO_ROOT, timeout=120,\n100:     )\n101: \n102: \n103: def test_missing_startup_yaml_exits_with_error_code(tmp_path):\n104:     result = _run_runner_subprocess({\"PVF_STARTUP_CONFIG\": str(tmp_path / \"missing.yaml\")})\n105:     assert result.returncode == 2\n106:     assert \"not found\" in result.stderr\n107: \n108: \n109: def test_bad_shell_module_exits_with_error_code(tmp_path):\n110:     config = tmp_path / \"bad_shell.yaml\"\n111:     config.write_text(\"application:\\n  shell_module: src.no_such_shell_module\\n\")\n112:     result = _run_runner_subprocess({\"PVF_STARTUP_CONFIG\": str(config)})\n113: ------------------------------------\n\ndef _app_with_route(*, guard=None, tags=None, path=\"/thing\"):\n    app = FastAPI()\n    router = APIRouter()\n    if guard is None:\n        @router.get(path, tags=tags or [])\n        async def thing():\n            return True\n    else:\n        @router.get(path, tags=tags or [])\n        async def thing_guarded(ctx=Depends(guard)):\n            return    verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(), \"session\")\n\n\ndef test_session_app_accepts_session_guard():\n    verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(guard=get_current_user), verify_app_dependency_integrity(_app_with_route(), \"external\")\n\n\ndef test_noauth_and_share_apps_forbid_guards():\n    for target in (\"noauth\", \"share\"):\n        with \"external\"}\n    assert MOUNT_PREFIXES == {\"noauth\": \"/auth-ws\", \"session\": \"/ws\", \"share\": \"/ext-ws\", \"external\": \"/api\"}\n    mounted_prefixes = {getattr(route, \"path\", None) for {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787317755648,"sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call-41ef18a1-9432-4d6c-b19a-69cd06abfcba-28","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/docs/PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage.MD","limit":120},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/docs/PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage.MD</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: # PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage\n2: \n3: A stand-up guide for senior engineers adopting the Pratt Ventures Framework. Companion to the short overview in [`README.md`](README.md).\n4: \n5: pvf is a multi-tenant FastAPI + SQLModel kernel: session JWT auth, customer/user admin, outbound email, share links, signed-header external APIs with webhook delivery, Stripe hooks, branding image storage, structured logging, and schema extension points. The runner never imports application modules by name; the application binds through a shell module named in YAML.\n6: \n7: ---\n8: \n9: ## Table of contents\n10: \n11: 1. [Design rules](#1-design-rules)\n12: 2. [Process map](#2-process-map)\n13: 3. [Configuration planes](#3-configuration-planes)\n14: 4. [Runner startup sequence](#4-runner-startup-sequence)\n15: 5. [Application handoff](#5-application-handoff)\n16: 6. [Four FastAPI apps and DI integrity](#6-four-fastapi-apps-and-di-integrity)\n17: 7. [Feature switches](#7-feature-switches)\n18: 8. [Hooks](#8-hooks)\n19: 9. [Schema extensions](#9-schema-extensions)\n20: 10. [Services (`pvf_services`)](#10-services-pvf_services)\n21: 11. [Share links](#11-share-links)\n22: 12. [External API and the watcher](#12-external-api-and-the-watcher)\n23: 13. [Email, Stripe, branding](#13-email-stripe-branding)\n24: 14. [Models, Alembic, database](#14-models-alembic-database)\n25: 15. [Stand-up checklist](#15-stand-up-checklist)\n26: 16. [Minimal application shell (all features on)](#16-minimal-application-shell-all-features-on)\n27: 17. [Import policy and pitfalls](#17-import-policy-and-pitfalls)\n28: \n29: ---\n30: \n31: ## 1. Design rules\n32: \n33: - **pvf never imports the application.** The runner loads the shell module named in YAML (`importlib`). Application code reaches pvf through published entry points only.\n34: - **Two config planes.** Deployment-fixed switches live in `pvf_app_startup.yaml` and are injected read-only. Secrets, URLs, and tuning live in `.env` / process environment.\n35: - **Handoff is a single callable.** `app_startup(invocation: PvfInvocation) -> str | None`. Return `None` to continue; return a string or raise to abort (exit 4).\n36: - **Hooks, not subclassing.** Application logic for shares, branding, and webhooks is registered on `invocation.hooks`. The watcher process binds the same hooks via `register_app_hooks`.\n37: - **Auth is composition-enforced.** Every route on each of the four apps is checked at boot (`dependency_integrity`). Missing or illegal guards fail startup with a listed violation.\n38: \n39: Published entry points (do not reach past these):\n40: \n41: | Entry point | Role |\n42: |---|---|\n43: | `pvf.pvf_app_runner` | Executable ASGI server (`fastapi dev` / `fastapi run`) |\n44: | `pvf.pvf_get_alembic_config` | Schema probe used by `src/alembic/env.py` |\n45: | `pvf.utils.pvf_services` | **Only** non-REST import surface |\n46: | `pvf.pvf_invocation` | `PvfInvocation`, `PvfAppTarget`, `PvfHookRegistry`, `PvfResolvedEntity` |\n47: | `pvf.config.pvf_config_settings` | `PvfGlobalSettings`, `pvf_settings` |\n48: | `pvf.depends.*` | `SessionDep`, `UserAccessDep` (+ admin), `WebServiceDep` |\n49: | `pvf.db.models.*` | Framework tables for queries and FKs |\n50: \n51: ---\n52: \n53: ## 2. Process map\n54: \n55: Three processes share YAML + env + hooks. Only the runner composes HTTP.\n56: \n57: ```\n58: pvf_app_startup.yaml ──► pvf_app_runner          ASGI: four apps + SPA + Stripe\n59:                     ├──► pvf_get_alembic_config  metadata + DSN for migrations\n60:                     └──► <app>_watcher           poll + signed webhook delivery\n61:                               ▲\n62:                               └── shell.register_app_hooks (same bindings)\n63: ```\n64: \n65: | Process | Loads shell | Calls `app_startup` | HTTP | Models |\n66: |---|---|---|---|---|\n67: | Runner | yes, then `entry_point` | yes | yes | core + **feature-gated** |\n68: | Alembic | `register_schema_extensions` only | no | no | **all** pvf + `models_module` |\n69: | Watcher | `register_app_hooks` only | no | no | all pvf + `models_module` |\n70: \n71: Alembic always migrates the full pvf schema so flipping a feature switch at runtime does not require a new migration.\n72: \n73: ---\n74: \n75: ## 3. Configuration planes\n76: \n77: ### 3.1 Startup YAML — deployment-fixed\n78: \n79: Path: application `src/` root (`src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`). Override with `PVF_STARTUP_CONFIG`. Template: `src/pvf/example_pvf_app_startup.yaml`.\n80: \n81: ```yaml\n82: application:\n83:   shell_module: src.app_shell              # imported; must not import models at module level\n84:   entry_point: app_startup                 # callable(PvfInvocation) -> str | None\n85:   models_module: src.db.models.bootstrap   # alembic + watcher table registration\n86: env_file: .env                             # python-dotenv; process env wins\n87: features:\n88:   activate_stripe_integration: true\n89:   activate_branding_image_store: true\n90:   activate_share_links: true\n91:   activate_external_api: true\n92:   activate_customer_communication: true\n93: share:\n94:   object_actions: [not_set, review, comment]   # share types you offer\n95:   access_operations: [not_set, view, comment]  # operations journaled per access\n96: ```\n97: \n98: `apply_startup_config` writes these into `pvf_settings` via `_inject_startup_values`. They are **ClassVars** with a `__setattr__` guard — `.env` and ordinary assignment cannot set them.\n99: \n100: | YAML | Settings ClassVar |\n101: |---|---|\n102: | `features.activate_stripe_integration` | `ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION` |\n103: | `features.activate_branding_image_store` | `ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE` |\n104: | `features.activate_share_links` | `ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS` |\n105: | `features.activate_external_api` | `ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API` |\n106: | `features.activate_customer_communication` | `ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION` |\n107: | `share.object_actions` | `SHARE_OBJECT_ACTIONS` |\n108: | `share.access_operations` | `SHARE_ACCESS_OPERATIONS` |\n109: \n110: Share vocabularies are normalized to `list[str]` (`normalize_str_list`). App-level enums are fine at your boundary; pvf stores strings.\n111: \n112: ### 3.2 Env file — runtime\n113: \n114: Ingested after YAML load, `override=False` (process environment wins). Template: repo-root `example.env`. Ship a real `.env`; the runner **exits 2** unless `UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK=1`.\n115: \n116: Application settings subclass `PvfGlobalSettings` and call `merge_pvf_settings_into(settings)` at the top of `app_startup` so the app instance carries the same YAML-owned values.\n117: \n118: Material groups on `PvfGlobalSettings` (all overridable from env except startup-owned ClassVars):\n119: \n120: | Group | Examples |\n\n(Showing lines 1-120 of 609. Use offset=121 to continue.)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage\n\nA stand-up guide for senior engineers adopting the Pratt Ventures Framework. The runner never imports application modules by name; the application binds through a shell module named in YAML.\n\n---\n\n## Table of contents\n\n1. [Design rules](#1-design-rules)\n2.
