[Services (`pvf_services`)](#10-services-pvf_services)\n11. [Share links](#11-share-links)\n12. [External API and the watcher](#12-external-api-and-the-watcher)\n13. [Email, Stripe, branding](#13-email-stripe-branding)\n14. [Models, Alembic, database](#14-models-alembic-database)\n15. [Stand-up checklist](#15-stand-up-checklist)\n16. [Minimal application shell (all features on)](#16-minimal-application-shell-all-features-on)\n17. [Import policy and pitfalls](#17-import-policy-and-pitfalls)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Design rules\n\n- **pvf never imports the application.** The runner loads the shell module named in YAML (`importlib`). Application code reaches pvf through published entry points only.\n- **Two config planes.** Deployment-fixed switches live in `pvf_app_startup.yaml` and are injected read-only. Secrets, URLs, and tuning live in `.env` / process environment.\n- **Handoff is a single callable.** `app_startup(invocation: PvfInvocation) -> str | None`. Return `None` to continue; return a string or raise to abort (exit 4).\n- **Hooks, not subclassing.** Application logic for shares, branding, and webhooks is registered on `invocation.hooks`. The watcher process binds the same hooks via `register_app_hooks`.\n- **Auth is composition-enforced.** Every route on each of the four apps is checked at boot (`dependency_integrity`). Missing or illegal guards fail startup with a listed violation.\n\nPublished entry points (do not reach past these):\n\n| Entry point | Role |\n|---|---|\n| `pvf.pvf_app_runner` | Executable ASGI server (`fastapi dev` / `fastapi run`) |\n| `pvf.pvf_get_alembic_config` | Schema probe used by `src/alembic/env.py` |\n| `pvf.utils.pvf_services` | **Only** non-REST import surface |\n| `pvf.pvf_invocation` | `PvfInvocation`, `PvfAppTarget`, `PvfHookRegistry`, `PvfResolvedEntity` |\n| `pvf.config.pvf_config_settings` | `PvfGlobalSettings`, `pvf_settings` |\n| `pvf.depends.*` | `SessionDep`, `UserAccessDep` (+ admin), `WebServiceDep` |\n| `pvf.db.models.*` | Framework tables for queries and FKs |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Process map\n\nThree processes share YAML + env + hooks. Only the runner composes HTTP.\n\n```\npvf_app_startup.yaml ──► pvf_app_runner          ASGI: four apps + SPA + Stripe\n                    ├──► pvf_get_alembic_config  metadata + DSN for migrations\n                    └──► <app>_watcher           poll + signed webhook delivery\n                              ▲\n                              └── shell.register_app_hooks (same bindings)\n```\n\n| Process | Loads shell | Calls `app_startup` | HTTP | Models |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Runner | yes, then `entry_point` | yes | yes | core + **feature-gated** |\n| Alembic | `register_schema_extensions` only | no | no | **all** pvf + `models_module` |\n| Watcher | `register_app_hooks` only | no | no | all pvf + `models_module` |\n\nAlembic always migrates the full pvf schema so flipping a feature switch at runtime does not require a new migration.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Configuration planes\n\n### 3.1 Startup YAML — deployment-fixed\n\nPath: application `src/` root (`src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`). Override with `PVF_STARTUP_CONFIG`. Template: `src/pvf/example_pvf_app_startup.yaml`.\n\n```yaml\napplication:\n  shell_module: src.app_shell              # imported; must not import models at module level\n  entry_point: app_startup                 # callable(PvfInvocation) -> str | None\n  models_module: src.db.models.bootstrap   # alembic + watcher table registration\nenv_file: .env                             # python-dotenv; process env wins\nfeatures:\n  activate_stripe_integration: true\n  activate_branding_image_store: true\n  activate_share_links: true\n  activate_external_api: true\n  activate_customer_communication: true\nshare:\n  object_actions: [not_set, review, comment]   # share types you offer\n  access_operations: [not_set, view, comment]  # operations journaled per access\n```\n\n`apply_startup_config` writes these into `pvf_settings` via `_inject_startup_values`. They are **ClassVars** with a `__setattr__` guard — `.env` and ordinary assignment cannot set them.\n\n| YAML | Settings ClassVar |\n|---|---|\n| `features.activate_stripe_integration` | `ACTIVATE_STRIPE_INTEGRATION` |\n| `features.activate_branding_image_store` | `ACTIVATE_BRANDING_IMAGE_STORE` |\n| `features.activate_share_links` | `ACTIVATE_SHARE_LINKS` |\n| `features.activate_external_api` | `ACTIVATE_EXTERNAL_API` |\n| `features.activate_customer_communication` | `ACTIVATE_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION` |\n| `share.object_actions` | `SHARE_OBJECT_ACTIONS` |\n| `share.access_operations` | `SHARE_ACCESS_OPERATIONS` |\n\nShare vocabularies are normalized to `list[str]` (`normalize_str_list`). App-level enums are fine at your boundary; pvf stores strings.\n\n### 3.2 Env file — runtime\n\nIngested after YAML load, `override=False` (process environment wins). Template: repo-root `example.env`. Ship a real `.env`; the runner **exits 2** unless `UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK=1`.\n\nApplication settings subclass `PvfGlobalSettings` and call `merge_pvf_settings_into(settings)` at the top of `app_startup` so the app instance carries the same YAML-owned values.\n\nMaterial groups on `PvfGlobalSettings` (all overridable from env except startup-owned ClassVars):\n\n| Group | Examples |","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":120,"totalLines":609,"truncated":true}},"title":"src/pvf/docs/PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage.MD","time":{"start":1787317754962,"end":1787317755606}},"id":"prt_024709c42001KhVyB54GvmWwZQ","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","messageID":"msg_0246f9d49002OhWU5c83IfeEQN"}}

Docs\")\n\n    # mounts: API prefixes first, static last\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"external\"], app_external, name=\"external_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"noauth\"], app_noauth, name=\"noauth_api\")","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py","text":"\n    # root status (reports the application's identity/version when supplied by the shell)\n    @root_app.get(\"/status\")\n    async def root_status():\n        return {\"service\": f\"{invocation.application_name or pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME} Service UI API\",\n                \"version\": invocation.application_version or pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION,\n                \"status\": \"API Available\"}\n\n    # aggregate session-surface contract (root + noauth + session + share)\n    @root_app.get(\"/session/docs.json\", include_in_schema=False)\n    async def sessions_openapi():\n        return JSONResponse(build_sessions_openapi())\n\n    @root_app.get(\"/session/docs\", include_in_schema=False)\n    async def sessions_docs():\n        return get_swagger_ui_html(openapi_url=\"/session/docs.json\", title=\"Session UI API - Docs\")\n\n    # mounts: API prefixes first, static last\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"external\"], app_external, name=\"external_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"noauth\"], app_noauth, name=\"noauth_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"session\"], app_session, name=\"session_api\")\n    root_app.mount(MOUNT_PREFIXES[\"share\"], app_share, name=\"share_api\")\n\n    root_app.mount(\"/static\", StaticFiles(directory=_STATIC_DIR), name=\"static\")\n    root_app.mount(\"/\", StaticFiles(directory=_STATIC_CLIENT_DIR), name=\"static_client_files\")\n\n    composed_apps = {\n        \"root\": root_app,\n        \"noauth\": app_noauth,\n        \"session\": app_session,\n        \"share\": app_share,\n        \"external\": app_external,\n    }\n\n    from .utils.utils_show import show_vars_semi\n\n    show_vars_semi(\"pvf_app_runner is running\", app_name=pvf_settings.APPLICATION_NAME, pvf_vers=pvf_settings.PVF_VERSION, pid=os.getpid())\n    return root_app\n\n\napp = build_app()","lineStart":370,"lineEnd":410,"totalLines":410,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py","time":{"start":1787317755534,"end":1787317756556}},"id":"prt_024709e47001IrrSM48cL5B5tj","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","messageID":"msg_0246f9d49002OhWU5c83IfeEQN"}}

Pytest short-circuits both backends.\n433: - Throttles: per dest/hour, per dest/24h, per remote IP (stricter when unauthenticated).\n434: \n435: Framework auth types (`AuthRelatedOutboundEmailType`): password reset, customer activation variants, new-user welcome, magic access key. Share invitation types are **yours**; return them from the email dispatcher hooks.\n436: \n437: ### Stripe\n438: \n439: Feature on → root routes:\n440: \n441: - `POST /hook-stripe-events/{hook_str}`\n442: - `POST /hook-stripe-events-sandbox/{hook_str}`\n443: \n444: `hook_str` is `STRIPE_HOOK_STR_PRIMARY` or a per-customer hook id. Authorization is the Stripe signature (`STRIPE_ENDPOINT_SECRET_PRIMARY`), not session/API guards. pvf persists events and subscriber transactions.\n445: \n446: ### Branding\n447: \n448: Feature on → session upload/get/delete for customer- and entity-scoped images; share-app read endpoints that resolve the entity via `entity_resolver` + the share cookie. Images stored in pvf tables, not the filesystem.\n449: \n450: ---\n451: \n452: ## 14. Models, Alembic, database\n453: \n454: **pvf tables (core):** `customer`, `user`, `userpasswords`, `userpasswordresettokens`, `sharelink` + access/magic-key, `apiaccessconfiguration`, `apiwebinvocationevent`, `emailactivitylog`, `applicationlogevent`, `usersessionlog`.\n455: \n456: **Feature tables:** `stripeevent`, `subscribertransaction`; branding image tables.\n457: \n458: **Your tables:** imported by `models_module`. That module should call `pvf_bootstrap.import_all_models()` then import app models, and expose `get_target_metadata()` → `SQLModel.metadata`.\n459: \n460: ```python\n461: # src/db/models/bootstrap.py\n462: from sqlmodel import SQLModel\n463: from ...pvf.db.models import pvf_bootstrap\n464: \n465: pvf_bootstrap.import_all_models()\n466: from .widgets import Widget  # noqa: F401\n467: \n468: def get_target_metadata():\n469:     return SQLModel.metadata\n470: ```\n471: \n472: `src/alembic/env.py` is a shim over `get_alembic_runtime()`: YAML → env → inject → `register_schema_extensions` → all pvf models → `models_module` → metadata + DSN. `BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA` is forced `False` so alembic owns schema.\n473: \n474: SQLite `create_all` runs only when **not** prod **and** `BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA`. Real schema changes go through Alembic (Postgres in the normal dev sidecar).\n475: \n476: `DatabaseConnection` is a process-global engine. `get_next_session()` is for startup/watcher/log_event; request handlers use `SessionDep`.\n477: \n478: ---\n479: \n480: ## 15. Stand-up checklist\n481: \n482: 1. Copy pvf into `src/pvf/` (or depend on it in-tree). Repo root on `PYTHONPATH`.\n483: 2. `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` — shell, entry, models module, env file, **all features you want**, share vocabularies.\n484: 3. `.env` from `example.env` with `UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK=1`, DSN, JWT secrets, mail, Stripe as needed.\n485: 4. `src/config/config_settings.py` — `class GlobalSettings(PvfGlobalSettings)` + app `VERSION` / `APPLICATION_NAME`.\n486: 5. `src/app_shell.py` — `register_schema_extensions` (optional, no model imports), `register_app_hooks`, `app_startup`.\n487: 6. `src/db/models/bootstrap.py` — `import_all_models()` + app tables + `get_target_metadata`.\n488: 7. App routers: correct `PvfAppTarget` and the matching Depends.\n489: 8. Share payload routes on the share app; webhook builders in `register_app_hooks`.\n490: 9. Jinja and/or SendGrid templates for every email type you dispatch.\n491: 10. `src/<app>_watcher.py` from `example.application_watcher.py` if you enqueue callbacks.\n492: 11. Alembic: `src/alembic/env.py` already delegates; generate/run migrations after model or extension changes.\n493: 12. Launch: `fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py --host 0.0.0.0` (or the app’s start script). Confirm `/status`, `/session/docs`, `/api/docs`.\n494: \n495: ---\n496: \n497: ## 16. Pytest short-circuits both backends.\n- Throttles: per dest/hour, per dest/24h, per remote IP (stricter when unauthenticated).\n\nFramework auth types (`AuthRelatedOutboundEmailType`): password reset, customer activation variants, new-user welcome, magic access key. Share invitation types are **yours**; return them from the email dispatcher hooks.\n\n### Stripe\n\nFeature on → root routes:\n\n- `POST /hook-stripe-events/{hook_str}`\n- `POST /hook-stripe-events-sandbox/{hook_str}`\n\n`hook_str` is `STRIPE_HOOK_STR_PRIMARY` or a per-customer hook id. Authorization is the Stripe signature (`STRIPE_ENDPOINT_SECRET_PRIMARY`), not session/API guards. pvf persists events and subscriber transactions.\n\n### Branding\n\nFeature on → session upload/get/delete for customer- and entity-scoped images; share-app read endpoints that resolve the entity via `entity_resolver` + the share cookie. Images stored in pvf tables, not the filesystem.\n\n---\n\n## 14. Models, Alembic, database\n\n**pvf tables (core):** `customer`, `user`, `userpasswords`, `userpasswordresettokens`, `sharelink` + access/magic-key, `apiaccessconfiguration`, `apiwebinvocationevent`, `emailactivitylog`, `applicationlogevent`, `usersessionlog`.\n\n**Feature tables:** `stripeevent`, `subscribertransaction`; branding image tables.\n\n**Your tables:** imported by `models_module`. That module should call `pvf_bootstrap.import_all_models()` then import app models, and expose `get_target_metadata()` → `SQLModel.metadata`.\n\n```python\n# src/db/models/bootstrap.py\nfrom sqlmodel import SQLModel\nfrom ...pvf.db.models import pvf_bootstrap\n\npvf_bootstrap.import_all_models()\nfrom .widgets import Widget  # noqa: F401\n\ndef get_target_metadata():\n    return SQLModel.metadata\n```\n\n`src/alembic/env.py` is a shim over `get_alembic_runtime()`: YAML → env → inject → `register_schema_extensions` → all pvf models → `models_module` → metadata + DSN. `BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA` is forced `False` so alembic owns schema.\n\nSQLite `create_all` runs only when **not** prod **and** `BOOTSTRAP_SAMPLE_DATA`. Real schema changes go through Alembic (Postgres in the normal dev sidecar).\n\n`DatabaseConnection` is a process-global engine. `get_next_session()` is for startup/watcher/log_event; request handlers use `SessionDep`.\n\n---\n\n## 15. Stand-up checklist\n\n1. Copy pvf into `src/pvf/` (or depend on it in-tree). Repo root on `PYTHONPATH`.\n2. `src/pvf_app_startup.yaml` — shell, entry, models module, env file, **all features you want**, share vocabularies.\n3. `.env` from `example.env` with `UNIQUE_CONFIGURATION_CHECK=1`, DSN, JWT secrets, mail, Stripe as needed.\n4. `src/config/config_settings.py` — `class GlobalSettings(PvfGlobalSettings)` + app `VERSION` / `APPLICATION_NAME`.\n5. `src/app_shell.py` — `register_schema_extensions` (optional, no model imports), `register_app_hooks`, `app_startup`.\n6. `src/db/models/bootstrap.py` — `import_all_models()` + app tables + `get_target_metadata`.\n7. App routers: correct `PvfAppTarget` and the matching Depends.\n8. Share payload routes on the share app; webhook builders in `register_app_hooks`.\n9. Jinja and/or SendGrid templates for every email type you dispatch.\n10. `src/<app>_watcher.py` from `example.application_watcher.py` if you enqueue callbacks.\n11. Alembic: `src/alembic/env.py` already delegates; generate/run migrations after model or extension changes.\n12. Launch: `fastapi dev src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py --host 0.0.0.0` (or the app’s start script). Confirm `/status`, `/session/docs`, `/api/docs`.\n\n---\n\n## 16. Minimal application shell (all features on)\n\nYAML as in [§3.1](#31-startup-yaml--deployment-fixed) with every `activate_*` true.\n\n```python\n# src/config/config_settings.py\nfrom ..pvf.config.pvf_config_settings import PvfGlobalSettings\n\nclass GlobalSettings(PvfGlobalSettings):\n    VERSION: str = \"0.1.0\"\n    APPLICATION_NAME: str = \"Widget App\"\n\nsettings = GlobalSettings()","lineStart":430,"lineEnd":509,"totalLines":609,"truncated":true}},"title":"src/pvf/docs/PVF Architecture, Configuration and Usage.MD","time":{"start":1787317765456,"end":1787317765568}},"id":"prt_02470c46d001XKhFwlXvjCfOJe","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","messageID":"msg_02470adc6002OOZOystkXZLqur"}}

{"type":"text","timestamp":1787317831381,"sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","part":{"id":"prt_024716be1001GvQiNdH26NfGAA","messageID":"msg_02470cb6e002tIiqKH0W6RHVWb","sessionID":"ses_fdb91863bffe8kfFnbf38rmfE4","type":"text","text":"## Plan: PVF pytest database connection\n\n### 1. Implementation steps\n\n1. **Add sibling test DSN on `PvfGlobalSettings`** (`src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py:153`)\n   - Add `DB_TEST_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING` immediately under `DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING`.\n   - Default: env override, else derive from the live DSN via `sqlalchemy.engine.make_url` by appending `_test` to `url.database` (Postgres `…/powerchoice` → `…/powerchoice_test`; SQLite `local.db` → `local_test.db`). Host/user/password/query unchanged.\n   - Add `active_db_url()` → test DSN iff `PYTEST_ACTIVE`, else live DSN.\n\n2. **Route runtime connections through `active_db_url()`**\n   - `src/pvf/db/connect.py`: `get_engine()` / sqlite `create_all` / log line use `settings.active_db_url()`.\n   - `src/pvf/pvf_app_runner.py:182`: probe `pvf_settings.active_db_url()`.\n\n3. **Set `PYTEST_ACTIVE` before any settings/app import** (`src/tests/conftest.py`)\n   - First lines (same pattern as `ENABLE_SHARE_*`): `os.environ[\"PYTEST_ACTIVE\"] = \"1\"`.\n   - Keep assigning `True` on both `settings` and `pvf_settings`.\n   - This is what makes pytest hit the test DSN: `build_app()` runs at import and reads `PYTEST_ACTIVE` then.\n\n4. **Alembic generate vs apply**\n   - `get_alembic_runtime()` **always** returns live `DB_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING` (autogenerate / `revision` inspect primary only). Do not honor `PYTEST_ACTIVE` here.\n   - `run_migrations_online`: if the command is `upgrade` or `downgrade`, apply the same revision to **both** URLs with **ephemeral engines** (do not reuse `DatabaseConnection` — it is a process-global singleton). `revision --autogenerate`, `current`, `heads` stay on primary.\n   - `scripts/migrations/run-migrations.sh` / `rollback-one-migration.sh`: keep as single `alembic` invocations; dual apply lives in PVF. Before apply, ensure the Postgres test DB exists (`CREATE DATABASE` on the live server if missing). Fresh compose volumes: add an init SQL next to the sidecar so `powerchoice_test` is created with `powerchoice`.\n   - `generate-migration.sh` unchanged.\n\n5. **Config templates and docs**\n   - `example.env`: sibling `DB_TEST_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING=…/powerchoice_test` under the live DSN.\n   - `AGENTS.md`: tests use a distinct DB (same server in dev); `PYTEST_ACTIVE=1` selects it; migrate/rollback both; generate against live only. Fix “hit the real configured DB (not isolated)”.\n   - Short notes: `README.md` test blurb; PVF architecture §3.2 Database row + §14.\n\n6. **Tests**\n   - Assert `PYTEST_ACTIVE` is true and `DatabaseConnection.get_engine().url.database` ends with `_test` (or equals the test DSN).\n   - `test_pvf_alembic_config.py`: `runtime.db_url` remains the **live** DSN even under pytest.\n   - `test_database_failure_exits_with_error_code`: subprocess must set `PYTEST_ACTIVE=0` (and/or both DSNs to the bad URL) so inherited pytest env does not route to the good test DB.\n   - Run `src/.venv/bin/pytest src/tests/test_pvf_alembic_config.py src/tests/test_pvf_app_runner.py` plus a small API module after migrating the test DB.\n   - Bump `settings.VERSION` last segment (`src/config/config_settings.py`) per AGENTS.md.\n\n---\n\n### 2. Clarifying questions\n\nProceed with these defaults unless you say otherwise:\n\n1. **Test DB creation?** Recommend: `run-migrations.sh` creates `powerchoice_test` if missing; compose init SQL for new volumes. Pytest does **not** auto-create (fail with a clear DSN error).\n2. **Dual Alembic in Python vs scripts?** Recommend: dual apply inside `run_migrations_online` for upgrade/downgrade so raw `alembic upgrade head` stays in sync. Scripts stay one-liners.\n3. **Config key name?** Recommend: `DB_TEST_PATH_OR_CONNECTION_STRING` (mirrors live key). Override in `.env`; default is live DSN + `_test` on the database name.\n4. **Existing local `.env`?** Derived default covers it if the new key is omitted. `example.env` still shows the explicit sibling value.\n\n---\n\n### 3. Options and trade-offs\n\n| Topic | Options | Recommendation |\n|---|---|---|\n| **DSN selection** | (A) Parallel env key + `PYTEST_ACTIVE` switch. (B) Always rewrite live DSN in pytest. (C) Separate `.env.test`. | **A.** Matches “similarly named and positioned”; overrideable if test DB is on another host later. |\n| **When `PYTEST_ACTIVE` is set** | (A) `os.environ` at top of conftest. (B) pytest plugin / `pytest.ini` (needs `pytest-env`). (C) Object assignment after settings import (today). | **A.** Engine is created at `app` import; env must be set first. No extra plugin. |\n| **Dual migrate** | (A) PVF `run_migrations_online` loops both URLs. (B) Shell loops `alembic -x target=test`. (C) Two alembic.ini sections. | **A.** Direct `alembic upgrade` cannot forget the test DB. Autogenerate stays on primary. |\n| **Create `_test` DB** | (A) Migration script + compose init. (B) Pytest `CREATE DATABASE`. (C) Docs only. | **A.** Official image only creates `POSTGRES_DB=powerchoice`; existing volumes need an ensure step. Avoid CREATEDB from every pytest process. |\n\n**Out of scope:** e2e / running server / OpenAPI extract / watcher stay on the live DSN (`PYTEST_ACTIVE` unset). Production must not set `PYTEST_ACTIVE`.","time":{"start":1787317808102,"end":1787317831277}}}
